def coordinate_descent(p0, t_start, budget, top_k=200, n_grid=10, n_golden=10, recompute_every=500, beta=200):
    p=normalize_positive(p0)
    b=conv_self(p); current_best=2*len(p)*b.max(); best_p=p.copy(); best_barr=b.copy()
    no_improve_count=0
    updates=0
    while time < t_start+budget:
       # recompute b if needed? initially given; after recompute_every updates.
       if updates % recompute_every == 0 and updates>0: b=conv_self(p); bmax=b.max()
       # get g
       bmax=b.max()
       if 2*n*bmax < best - tol: # current better
       # active weights: Use LSE on b only
       w, L = lse_weights_b(b, beta)
       g = conv_revp_w(p,w) - L
       g -= np.dot(p,g)
       order = np.argsort(np.abs(g))[::-1][:top_k]
       # shuffle? Maybe sort by score but randomize within.
       sweep_improved=False
       for i in order:
          if time check
          x0=p[i]
          upper = ... maybe p99 global computed each sweep.
          newx, newb, newmax, improved = optimize_coordinate(p,b,i,upper,n_grid,n_golden)
          if improved:
             p = new? optimize returns p and b? To avoid copies? We'll modify in place and return max.
             updates +=1
             if updates % recompute==0: b=conv_self(p)
             if 2*n*bmax_new < best: update best
             sweep_improved=True
       if not sweep_improved:
           # broaden: try top 2*top_k or random 100
           order2 = np.concatenate([order, random indices]) unique
           for ...
       # beta *=1.5? As converge, focus active: beta = min(5000, beta*1.5)
       beta = min(5000,beta*1.5)
    return best_score,best_p
```

Optimize coordinate implementation details with in-place update:
```
def optimize_coordinate_inplace(p,b,i,upper,n_grid,n_golden):
    n=len(p); m=2*n-1
    x0=p[i]
    current_max=b.max()
    R=1-x0
    if R<1e-14: return False, current_max
    if upper is None: upper=min(1.0,max(4*x0, 10*?))
    # Build arrays:
    other = b.copy()
    diag = 2*i
    if diag < m: other[diag] -= x0*x0
    js = np.arange(n)
    valid = js != i
    ss = i + js[valid]
    other[ss] -= 2*x0*p[valid]
    # due numerical: other[other<0 & small]=0; but if negative large due previous errors, clip all negative to 0
    np.maximum(other,0,out=other)
    N=1.0/R; N2=N*N
    gamma = N2*other
    beta = -2.0*gamma
    # Need alpha start gamma then add diag/cross:
    alpha = gamma.copy()
    # cross modifications:
    ec = 2.0*N*p[valid]
    alpha[ss] -= ec
    beta[ss] += ec
    if diag < m: alpha[diag] += 1.0
    # Now evaluate. We can define local evaluate? no closures, but inside function can have inner? Rule says no nested? It says make all helper functions top level and no closures from function nesting. Inside optimize, cannot define `def eval` nested. We can write loops inline for grid and golden, calling a top-level `max_quad(maxb? x, alpha,beta,gamma)`? But that would pass arrays each time. Good: top-level `quad_max(x, A,B,C): return (C + B*x + A*x*x).max()`. It creates array. For speed, inline expression in loop.
```
But `quad_max` top-level can be used. It returns max.

def fft_size(m):
    r=1
    while r<m: r <<=1
    return r

`conv_self(a)`:
```
n=len(a)
m=2*n-1
N=1 << (m-1).bit_length()
A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
b=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:m]
# b may have tiny negatives; set b[np.abs(b)<1e-15]=0? maybe not each time.
return b
```

`conv_revp_w(a,w)`:
```
n=len(a)
m=3*n-2
N=1 << (m-1).bit_length()
A=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N)
W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
return np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)[n-1:n-1+n]
```
Could cache FFT of p? w changes; no.

`normalize_p(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0)
a=np.clip(a,0,None)
s=a.sum()
if s<=1e-12: return np.full_like(a,1/len(a))
return a/s
```
For floor, do `np.maximum(a,1e-300)`. But if many zeros, sum tiny? okay. Use min 0? For coordinate descent zeros okay. For L-BFGS maybe floor. For coordinate p can be zero.

`lse_weights(b,beta)`:
```
bmax=float(b.max())
scaled=beta*(b-bmax)
exps=np.exp(scaled)
s=float(exps.sum())
w=exps/s
L=bmax+np.log(s)/beta
return w,L
```
If beta very large, scaled can be -inf? For b-bmax negative large*beta; exp underflows to0, but max exp=1. sum>0. Good. If b has NaN? no.

`quad_max(x,A,B,C)`: top-level:
```
def quad_max(x,A,B,C):
    return float((C + B*x + A*x*x).max())
```
Could inline for speed; top-level no closures.

def coordinate_descent(p0, t_start, budget, top_k=200, n_grid=12, n_golden=12, beta=100.0, no_improve_time=120.0, recompute_every=200):
    n=len(p0); p=normalize_p(p0)
    b=conv_self(p)
    best_max = float(b.max()); best_score=2*n*best_max; best_p=p.copy(); best_b=b.copy()
    last_improve=time.monotonic()
    updates=0
    beta_current=beta
    deadline=t_start+budget
    p99 = float(np.quantile(p,0.99)); max_p=float(p.max())
    no_sweeps_no_imp=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2:
        if updates >0 and updates % recompute_every ==0:
            b=conv_self(p)
            bsum=b.sum(); if >... b/=bsum
            np.maximum(b,0,out=b)
            updates=0
        bmax=float(b.max())
        if bmax < best_max - 1e-14:
            best_max=bmax; best_score=2*n*bmax; best_p=p.copy(); last_improve=time.monotonic()
        # if no improvement for no_improve_time: break? or continue with restart? inside function maybe break.
        if time.monotonic()-last_improve > no_improve_time:
            break
        w,L=lse_weights(b,beta_current)
        g=conv_revp_w(p,w)-L
        g -= float(np.dot(p,g))
        # top_k maybe dynamic
        k=min(n, top_k)
        order=np.abs(g)
        # get top k indices: use argpartition for speed
        if k < n:
           part=np.argpartition(order, k)[:k]
           order_idx=part[np.argsort(order[part])]
        else: order_idx=np.argsort(order)[::-1]
        # shuffle? To avoid same order if g similar. Could add random noise? rng? Need rng top-level? We can pass rng? Function args can include seed. But top-level no closures. We can create rng inside coordinate_descent: `rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)` but seed required? Use time? deterministic. Add parameter `seed=1`.
        # randomize top: use rng.permutation(order_idx)
        sweep_improved=False
        for i in order_idx:
             if time > deadline-2: break
             # Maybe if p[i] very close zero and g low? still.
             # compute upper using max_p,p99
             x0=p[int(i)]
             upper = ... function? top-level `coord_upper(x0,max_p,n)`.
             improved, new_max = optimize_coordinate(p,b,i,upper,n_grid,n_golden)
             if improved:
                 updates +=1
                 sweep_improved=True
                 if new_max < best_max - 1e-14:
                     best_max=new_max; best_score=2*n*new_max; best_p=p.copy(); last_improve=time.monotonic()
                 # recompute max_p,p99 less frequent? They change; compute every 100 updates or after sweep.
        # after sweep, update p99/max_p if updates>0
        if sweep_improved:
             no_sweeps=0
             # update beta maybe if many improvements: beta_current = max(50, beta_current*0.9?) Actually as improving, keep/increase? We want focus: increase beta if drops; maybe beta_current*=1.3 up to 5000.
             beta_current = min(5000.0, beta_current*1.25)
        else:
             no_sweeps +=1
             beta_current = beta_current*1.5 # narrow
             if no_sweeps >= 3:
                 # break to allow outer restarts
                 break
        # Every 50 updates or each sweep, recompute p99/max

             max_p=float(p.max()); p99=float(np.quantile(p,0.99))
    # final exact
    sc=score_p_from_conv? Use b recompute
    b=conv_self(p)
    sc=2*n*b.max()
    if sc<best_score: update
    return best_score,best_p

`perturb_p(p, rng, kind, strength)`: top-level.
- If kind==0 additive multiplicative noise: `q=p*(1+strength*rng.standard_normal(n)); q=np.clip(q,0,None); q /= q.sum()`.
- kind==1 spectral: compute A=np.fft.rfft(q, N), amp=1+strength*rng.normal, phase=strength*rng.normal, A*=amp*exp(1j phase); q=irfft(A,N)[:n]; clip; normalize. Need ensure sum positive.
- kind==2 block swap: q=p.copy(); swap 2-4 blocks length 10-200; normalize (sum same).
- kind==3 anti-palin: u=rng.standard_normal(n); u-=u.mean(); u = u - u[::-1]? Then q=p + strength* u * p.max()? clip; normalize.
- kind==4 resample: length factor passed. Actually separate.

Resampling:
```
def resample_sequence(p, new_len):
   x_old=np.arange(len(p)); x_new=np.linspace(0,len(p)-1,new_len)
   q=np.interp(x_new,x_old,p)
   q=np.clip(q,0,None); s=q.sum(); return q/s
```

Detailed functions:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _fft_size(m):
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```
If m<=1? For m=1, (0).bit_length? (m-1)=0 ->0 ->1<<0=1. But n>=1. For m maybe 0? no.

`conv_self(a)`:
```
def conv_self(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n==1:
        return np.array([a[0]*a[0]], dtype=float)
    m=2*n-1
    N=1 << (m-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    b=np.fft.irfft(A*A, N)[:m]
    # clean small imaginary? irfft real
    b[b<0] = 0? This may be done but if small negative due precision; if all positive. Use `np.maximum(b,0,out=b)`.
    return b
```
Clipping negatives in conv_self may break sum for LSE but small. Do it.

`conv_revp_w(a,w)`:
```
n=len(a); m=3*n-2; N=1 << (m-1).bit_length(); return np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N)*np.fft.rfft(w,N),N)[n-1:n-1+n]
```
No clip.

`normalize_p(a)`:
```
def normalize_p(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
       return np.full(a.size, 1.0/a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a/s
```
Use a.size maybe 0? If input empty, return [1]? But parent.

`score_p_normalized(p)`:
```
def score_p(p):
    p=normalize_p(p)
    b=conv_self(p)
    return float(2*len(p)*b.max())
```

`lse_weights(b,beta)`:
```
def lse_weights(b,beta):
    bmax=float(b.max())
    # if beta large, scaled
    exps=np.exp(beta*(b-bmax))
    s=float(exps.sum())
    if s <=0: w=np.zeros_like(b); w[np.argmax(b)]=1; L=bmax
    else: w=exps/s; L=bmax+float(np.log(s))/beta
    return w,L
```

`quad_max(x,A,B,C)` top-level.

`coord_upper(x0,max_p,n)`:
```
def coord_upper(x0,max_p,n):
    base = max(5.0*max_p, 2.0/n, 1e-6)
    upper = max(base, 4.0*x0, 2.0*max_p)
    if upper <= x0:
       upper = min(1.0, x0*1.5 + base)
    return min(1.0, upper)
```
If x0=0 and max_p very small? base.

`optimize_coordinate(p,b,i,upper,n_grid,n_golden)`:
Need return `(improved, new_bmax)`. It modifies p,b. Let's code:
```
def optimize_coordinate(p,b,i,upper,n_grid,n_golden):
    n=len(p)
    m=2*n-1
    x0=float(p[i])
    current_max=float(b.max())
    R=1.0-x0
    if R < 1e-14:
        return False, current_max
    if upper <= 0: upper=1e-6
    if upper > 1.0: upper=1.0
    # build arrays
    A_other = b.copy()
    diag=2*i
    if diag < m:
        A_other[diag] -= x0*x0
    js=np.arange(n)
    valid=js != i
    ss=i+js[valid]
    A_other[ss] -= 2.0*x0*p[valid]
    np.maximum(A_other,0.0,out=A_other)
    Ninv=1.0/R
    N2=Ninv*Ninv
    C=N2*A_other
    B=-2.0*C
    A=C.copy()
    ec=2.0*Ninv*p[valid]
    A[ss] -= ec
    B[ss] += ec
    if diag < m: A[diag] += 1.0
    # grid eval
    xs=np.linspace(0.0, upper, n_grid)
    # ensure x0 included? maybe if 0<x0<upper
    if x0 > 0.0 and x0 < upper:
        xs=np.concatenate((xs,[x0]))
    best_x=0.0
    best_val=quad_max(0.0,A,B,C)
    for x in xs[1:]:
       val=quad_max(float(x),A,B,C)
       if val < best_val:
           best_val=val; best_x=float(x)
    # if current in xs and better included? okay.
    if best_val >= current_max - 1e-14:
        return False, current_max
    # golden refine around interval
    # find position of best_x in xs: use searchsorted
    pos=int(np.searchsorted(xs, best_x, side='right'))-1
    pos=min(max(pos,0),len(xs)-1)
    if pos <= 0:
       lo=0.0; hi=float(xs[1]) if len(xs)>1 else upper
    elif pos >= len(xs)-1:
       lo=float(xs[-2]) if len(xs)>1 else 0.0; hi=upper
    else:
       lo=float(xs[pos-1]); hi=float(xs[pos+1])
    # sometimes best at current x0 near middle with adjacent interval narrow; okay.
    gr=(math.sqrt(5.0)-1.0)/2.0
    # If lo==hi skip
    if hi-lo > 1e-16:
       c1=hi-gr*(hi-lo)
       c2=lo+gr*(hi-lo)
       f1=quad_max(c1,A,B,C)
       f2=quad_max(c2,A,B,C)
       for _ in range(n_golden):
           if hi-lo <= 1e-16: break
           if f1 < f2:
               hi=c2; c2=c1; f2=f1; c1=hi-gr*(hi-lo); f1=quad_max(c1,A,B,C)
           else:
               lo=c1; c1=c2; f1=f2; c2=lo+gr*(hi-lo); f2=quad_max(c2,A,B,C)
       if f1 < f2:
           best_x=c1; best_val=f1
       else:
           best_x=c2; best_val=f2
    # final check perhaps endpoints
    for xv in (0.0, upper, x0):
        val=quad_max(float(xv),A,B,C)
        if val < best_val: best_val=val; best_x=float(xv)
    if best_val < current_max - 1e-14:
       # apply
       factor=(1.0-best_x)/R
       p *= factor
       p[i]=best_x
       s=p.sum()
       if s > 0: p /= s
       b = C + B*best_x + A*best_x*best_x
       np.maximum(b,0.0,out=b)
       bs=b.sum()
       if bs > 1e-300:
           b /= bs
       return True, float(b.max())
    return False, current_max
```
Important: `A_other` variable named `A_other`, then `A` for quad. No name conflict.
`xs` includes x0 maybe duplicate; fine. Could be unsorted if concatenate x0 at end? We did linear then append x0 at end if x0<upper; x0 may be after end? If upper > x0, x0 within, appending at end breaks sorted order (since x0 likely < upper but > all? Unless upper>=x0, x0 < upper, if appended at end, it's sorted because linear ends at upper, smaller x0 before upper? Actually if x0<upper, x0 belongs inside, appending at end places after upper in array, so sorted violated. For searchsorted interval, xs must sorted. Better create without x0 but grid includes enough, or include and sort unique.
```
xs = np.linspace(0, upper, n_grid)
if x0>0 and x0<upper:
   xs = np.concatenate([xs, [x0]])
   xs.sort()
```
Grid then not uniformly spaced but okay. For golden interval, use xs[pos-1],xs[pos+1] which may be not adjacent if x0 appended; but okay.
Set `n_grid` maybe includes endpoints; if x0=upper not add.
If n_golden=0? no.

Potential issue: `b = C + B*best_x + A*best_x*x` reassigns local b to new array; outer function's b variable not updated unless returned. In Python, assignment to `b` inside function local; passing b mutable and then `b = ...` creates local, not modify outer. If we do `b[:] = ...` it updates outer. We need update in place. Use `b_new = C + ...; b[:] = b_new`. Since outer coordinate_descent has b variable reference, in-place update works. Similarly p in-place. So in optimize, after compute: `b[:] = C + B*best_x + A*best_x*best_x`; then clip/normalize. Do not `b = ...`. However `A_other = b.copy()` at start uses current b; fine.
Return new max.

Potential issue: If p update `p *= factor` changes all coords including those used for `valid`? Arrays already built; okay. But we also have `A` computed; okay.

`coordinate_descent` needs to use local b variable; after optimize updates in place, b[...]. But if recompute, assign `b = conv_self(p)` local; this is fine because subsequent optimize gets this b. But if p updated in coordinate optimize, p reference same list.

Let's code coordinate_descent with local `b` and not relying on outer b update after recompute. Good.

`coordinate_descent` signature:
```
def coordinate_descent(p0, deadline, top_k=200, n_grid=12, n_golden=12, beta=100.0, no_improve_seconds=120.0, recompute_every=200, seed=1):
```
Inside:
```
p=normalize_p(p0)
b=conv_self(p)
best_max=float(b.max()); best_score=2*n*best_max; best_p=p.copy(); best_b=b.copy() # maybe not need b
last_improve=time.monotonic()
updates=0
beta_cur=beta
no_sweeps=0
max_p=float(p.max()); p99=float(np.quantile(p,0.99)) if n>1 else 1.0
rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
   if updates>0 and updates % recompute_every ==0:
       b=conv_self(p); np.maximum(...); updates=0
   bmax=float(b.max())
   if bmax < best_max - 1e-14:
       best_max=bmax; best_score=2*n*best_max; best_p=p.copy(); last_improve=time.monotonic()
   if time.monotonic()-last_improve > no_improve_seconds:
       break
   w,L=lse_weights(b,beta_cur)
   g=conv_revp_w(p,w)
   g -= L
   g -= float(np.dot(p,g))
   # optionally add tiny noise to g to diversify? g += 1e-6*rng.normal? Might destabilize. Maybe not.
   k=min(n, int(top_k))
   order=np.abs(g)
   part=np.argpartition(order, min(k,n)-1) if k<n else np.arange(n)
   if k<n:
       top=part[np.argsort(order[part])[::-1]][:k]
   else: top=np.argsort(order)[::-1]
   top=rng.permutation(top)
   improved_sweep=False
   for i in top:
      if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2: break
      i=int(i)
      x0=float(p[i])
      upper=coord_upper(x0,max_p,n)
      imp,newmax=optimize_coordinate(p,b,i,upper,n_grid,n_golden)
      if imp:
          improved_sweep=True
          updates +=1
          if newmax < best_max - 1e-14:
              best_max=newmax; best_score=2*n*newmax; best_p=p.copy(); last_improve=time.monotonic()
          # after update, bmax maybe newmax; if updates % 50==0 recompute p stats
          if updates % 50 ==0:
              max_p=float(p.max()); p99=float(np.quantile(p,0.99))
              if updates % recompute_every ==0:
                   b=conv_self(p); np.maximum(b,0,out=b); bs=b.sum(); b/=bs if ...; updates=0
   if improved_sweep:
       no_sweeps=0
       beta_cur = min(5000.0, beta_cur*1.2)
   else:
       no_sweeps +=1
       beta_cur = min(20000.0, beta_cur*1.5)
       if no_sweeps >= 3:
           break
# final
b=conv_self(p); bmax=float(b.max()); sc=2*n*bmax
if sc < best_score: best_score=sc; best_p=p.copy()
return best_score,best_p
```

`perturb_p(p, rng, kind, strength, new_len=None)`:
```
def perturb_p(p, rng, kind, strength=0.1):
   n=len(p); q=p.copy()
   if kind == 0: q = q*(1+strength*rng.standard_normal(n)); q=np.clip(q,0,None); s=q.sum(); if s>0: q/=s
   elif kind == 1: # spectral
       m=2*n-1? Use FFT of length 2n-1 or n? p length n, use circular DFT length N=1<<... maybe n? We want diverse. Let N=1<<(n-1).bit_length(); A=np.fft.rfft(q,N); amp=1+strength*rng.standard_normal(len(A)); phase=strength*0.5*rng.standard_normal(len(A)); A*=amp*np.exp(1j*phase); q=np.fft.irfft(A,N)[:n]; q=np.clip(q,0,None); normalize.
   elif kind == 2: # block swap
       for _ in range(3): bl=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//100), max(2,n//20))); i=int(rng.integers(0,n-bl)); j=int(rng.integers(0,n-bl)); if i!=j: q[i:i+bl],q[j:j+bl]=q[j:j+bl].copy(),q[i:i+bl].copy()
       normalize
   elif kind == 3:
       u=rng.standard_normal(n); u = u - u[::-1]; q = p + strength*u*p.max(); q=np.clip(q,0,None); normalize
   elif kind ==4:
       # resample to new_len maybe require param; better separate
   return q
```

return q/s

def resample_p(p, new_len):

Now, final zero/trim function:
```
def final_clean(p, best_score):
    p=normalize_p(p)
    best_q=p.copy(); best_sc=best_score
    maxp=float(p.max())
    thresholds=[0.0, 1e-14*maxp, 1e-12*maxp, 1e-10*maxp, 1e-8*maxp]
    candidates=[]
    for thr in thresholds:
       q=p.copy(); q[q<thr]=0
       # trim ends
       nz=np.nonzero(q>0)[0]
       if nz.size==0: continue
       q=q[nz[0]:nz[-1]+1]
       if q.size < 2: continue
       q=normalize_p(q)
       sc=score_p(q)
       if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_q=q.copy()
    # maybe try internal zero not trim? Already included.
    return best_sc,best_q
```

def propose_candidate(seed=2024, budget_s=2400.0):
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    eff=max(30.0,float(budget_s))-60.0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=None
    try:
       hs=height_sequence_1
       if hs is not None:
          parent=np.asarray(hs,dtype=float).ravel()
    except Exception:
          pass
    if parent is None or parent.size<16 or np.sum(np.clip(parent,0,None))<1e-8:
          parent=generate_fallback_parent(8192,rng)
    else:
          parent=np.clip(parent,0,None)
          if parent.size < 2048:
             # maybe resample to 4096
             parent=resample_p(parent, 4096)
    best_p=normalize_p(parent)
    best_score=score_p(best_p)
    print(...)
    # Phase1
    deadline1=t_start+min(eff*0.45, eff-120)
    sc,p1=coordinate_descent(best_p, deadline1, top_k=250, n_grid=12, n_golden=12, beta=100.0, no_improve_seconds=90.0, recompute_every=50, seed=11)
    if sc<best_score: best_score=sc; best_p=p1
    # Phase2 restarts
    deadline_restart=t_start+eff*0.75
    kinds=[0,2,1,3,0,1,2,3]
    n_rest=8
    for r in range(n_rest):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline_restart - 30: break
       alloc=(deadline_restart - max(time.monotonic(), t_start+eff*0.45)) / max(1, n_rest-r)
       alloc=min(alloc, 180.0)
       if alloc <40: break
       kind=kinds[r%len(kinds)]
       strength=0.05 + 0.05*(r%4)
       if kind==4: # but kinds not 4; add resample once r==4? 
       q=perturb_p(best_p,rng,kind,strength)
       sc=score_p(q)
       print...
       if sc < best_score*1.35 + 1e-8:
           cd_alloc=0.7*alloc
           sc2,q2=coordinate_descent(q, time.monotonic()+cd_alloc, top_k=150, n_grid=10, n_golden=10, beta=300.0, no_improve_seconds=20.0, recompute_every=30, seed=1000+r)
           if sc2<best_score: update
       # if r==4 do resample candidate from best_p instead? Could before loop.
```

Check `coord_upper`: uses `2.0/n`; if n=0? no.
```
def coord_upper(x0,max_p,n):
    base=max(5.0*max_p, 2.0/float(n), 1e-6)
    upper=max(base, 4.0*x0, 2.0*max_p)
    if upper <= x0:
        upper=min(1.0, x0*1.5 + base)
    if upper<=0: upper=1e-6
    return min(1.0, upper)
```
If x0 close1, upper=1.

`optimize_coordinate`: After applying p update, `p[i]=best_x` after `p *= factor`. But if p[i] was multiplied, then set. Good.
```
s=p.sum(); if s>0: p/=s
```
This scaling after setting p[i]=best_x renormalizes to exactly1; but then p not exactly of form x + scaled others sum because p[i] remains best_x? If we divide whole p by s, p[i] becomes best_x/s, not exactly best_x. This changes the update from intended formula and b formula (which assumed exact sum before small rounding). Better to avoid renormalizing p entirely, or ensure rounding error negligible. If we renormalize all, consistency with b formula broken slightly. We should not renormalize p or do it after b update? The scaling already makes sum 1 within roundoff. Renormalizing slightly changes p and b formula. The error tiny. But if best_x close0 and factor large, sum could be 0.999999, dividing changes p_i by 1e-6, maybe b changes ~1e-6, not huge but could affect PR. To keep consistency, do:
```
p *= factor
p[i] = best_x
# do not normalize p; but due rounding sum may be 1±eps.
s=p.sum()
if not np.isclose(s,1,rtol=1e-12, atol=1e-15):
   # scale p; this changes b slightly but okay, and recompute b next time. But for current b formula based on pre-scale p, small mismatch. We can if correction significant, recompute b instead of using formula.
```

```python
"""
Coordinate-descent search for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(self-convolution) / (sum)^2

The main novelty is an exact one-coordinate subproblem.  For a fixed
coordinate, after renormalizing the remaining mass, every diagonal of the
self-convolution is a quadratic polynomial in the new coordinate value.
Thus each coordinate update can be found by a fast 1-D line search without
repeating an FFT.  The convolution buffer is updated directly in O(n)
per accepted move, with periodic FFT re-synchronization to control drift.

The search is driven by an LSE/active-diagonal subgradient to choose
promising coordinates, plus a small number of diversified restarts and final
endpoint/zero cleaning.
"""

import numpy as np
import math
import time

_ARANGE_CACHE = {}


def get_arange(n):
    a = _ARANGE_CACHE.get(n)
    if a is None:
        a = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
        _ARANGE_CACHE[n] = a
    return a


def conv_self(a):
    """Linear self-convolution using real FFT, length 2n-1."""
    n = len(a)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([a[0] * a[0]], dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    fft_len = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_len)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, fft_len)[:m]
    np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
    return b


def conv_revp_w(a, w):
    """
    Return Sv_i = sum_s w_s a_{s-i}.
    Equivalent to convolution of reversed a with w, shifted to length n.
    """
    n = len(a)
    m = 3 * n - 2
    fft_len = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], fft_len)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, fft_len)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * W, fft_len)[n - 1:n - 1 + n]


def normalize_p(a):
    """Clip to non-negative and normalize to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    np.clip(a, 0.0, None, out=a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(max(1, a.size), 1.0 / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a /= s
    return a


def score_p(p):
    """Exact score for normalized positive sequence p."""
    p = normalize_p(p)
    b = conv_self(p)
    return float(2 * len(p) * max(0.0, b.max()))


def resample_p(p, new_len):
    """Resample a step function to a new length and renormalize."""
    p = normalize_p(p)
    new_len = int(max(1, new_len))
    old_n = len(p)
    if old_n == 1:
        return np.ones(new_len, dtype=np.float64) / new_len
    x_old = np.arange(old_n, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, old_n - 1.0, new_len)
    q = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    q = normalize_p(q)
    return q


def lse_weights(b, beta):
    """Softmax weights over diagonals for an LSE approximation to the max."""
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    bmax = float(b.max())
    if beta <= 0.0:
        w = np.full_like(b, 1.0 / b.size, dtype=np.float64)
        L = float(b.mean())
        return w, L
    scaled = beta * (b - bmax)
    exps = np.exp(scaled)
    s = float(exps.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(b)
        w[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        L = bmax
    else:
        w = exps / s
        L = bmax + float(np.log(s)) / beta
    return w, L


def quad_max(x, A, B, C):
    """Max over diagonals of C + B*x + A*x*x."""
    return float((C + B * x + A * x * x).max())


def coord_upper(x0, max_p, n):
    """Choose an upper endpoint for the 1-D coordinate search."""
    base = max(5.0 * max_p, 2.0 / float(n), 1e-6)
    upper = max(base, 4.0 * x0, 2.0 * max_p)
    if upper <= x0:
        upper = min(1.0, 1.5 * x0 + base)
    if upper <= 0.0:
        upper = base
    return min(1.0, upper)


def optimize_coordinate(p, b, i, upper, n_grid, n_golden):
    """
    Exact one-coordinate move while preserving the total sum.

    Updates p and b in place if the maximum diagonal decreases.
    Returns (improved, new_diagonal_max).
    """
    n = len(p)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    i = int(i)
    x0 = float(p[i])
    current_max = float(b.max())
    R = 1.0 - x0
    if R <= 1e-10:
        return False, current_max
    if not np.isfinite(upper) or upper <= 0.0:
        upper = 1e-6
    upper = min(1.0, upper)

    # A_s = convolution contribution of coordinates other than i.
    A_other = b.copy()
    diag = 2 * i
    if diag < m:
        A_other[diag] -= x0 * x0

    cross_all = i + get_arange(n)
    left = cross_all[:i] if i > 0 else None
    right = cross_all[i + 1:] if i + 1 < n else None

    if left is not None:
        A_other[left] -= (2.0 * x0) * p[:i]
    if right is not None:
        A_other[right] -= (2.0 * x0) * p[i + 1:]

    # A_other should be non-negative; small numerical negatives are harmless.
    np.maximum(A_other, 0.0, out=A_other)

    Ninv = 1.0 / R
    N2 = Ninv * Ninv

    C = N2 * A_other
    B = -2.0 * C
    A = C.copy()

    if left is not None:
        ec = (2.0 * Ninv) * p[:i]
        A[left] -= ec
        B[left] += ec
    if right is not None:
        ec = (2.0 * Ninv) * p[i + 1:]
        A[right] -= ec
        B[right] += ec
    if diag < m:
        A[diag] += 1.0

    # Grid search for a robust starting interval.
    xs = np.linspace(0.0, float(upper), int(n_grid))
    if x0 > 0.0 and x0 < upper:
        xs = np.concatenate((xs, np.array([x0], dtype=np.float64)))
        xs.sort()

    best_x = 0.0
    best_val = quad_max(0.0, A, B, C)
    for x in xs[1:]:
        val = quad_max(float(x), A, B, C)
        if val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_x = float(x)

    if not np.isfinite(best_val) or best_val >= current_max - 1e-14:
        return False, current_max

    # Golden refine around the best grid interval.
    pos = int(np.searchsorted(xs, best_x, side="right")) - 1
    pos = min(max(pos, 0), len(xs) - 1)
    if pos <= 0:
        lo = 0.0
        hi = float(xs[1]) if len(xs) > 1 else float(upper)
    elif pos >= len(xs) - 1:
        lo = float(xs[-2]) if len(xs) > 1 else 0.0
        hi = float(upper)
    else:
        lo = float(xs[pos - 1])
        hi = float(xs[pos + 1])

    if hi > lo and (hi - lo) > 1e-16:
        gr = (math.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0
        c1 = hi - gr * (hi - lo)
        c2 = lo + gr * (hi - lo)
        f1 = quad_max(c1, A, B, C)
        f2 = quad_max(c2, A, B, C)
        for _ in range(int(n_golden)):
            if hi - lo <= 1e-16:
                break
            if f1 < f2:
                hi = c2
                c2 = c1
                f2 = f1
                c1 = hi - gr * (hi - lo)
                f1 = quad_max(c1, A, B, C)
            else:
                lo = c1
                c1 = c2
                f1 = f2
                c2 = lo + gr * (hi - lo)
                f2 = quad_max(c2, A, B, C)
        if f1 < f2:
            best_x, best_val = c1, f1
        else:
            best_x, best_val = c2, f2

    # Endpoints are easy and sometimes optimal.
    for xv in (0.0, float(upper), x0):
        val = quad_max(float(xv), A, B, C)
        if val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_x = float(xv)

    if best_val < current_max - 1e-14:
        factor = (1.0 - best_x) / R
        p *= factor
        p[i] = best_x

        b[:] = C + B * best_x + A * best_x * best_x
        np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
        bs = float(b.sum())
        if bs > 1e-300:
            b /= bs

        # If normalization/rounding error in p is larger than expected,
        # resynchronize the convolution buffer exactly.
        ps = float(p.sum())
        if ps > 0.0 and abs(ps - 1.0) > 1e-10:
            p /= ps
            b[:] = conv_self(p)
            np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
            bs = float(b.sum())
            if bs > 1e-300:
                b /= bs

        return True, float(b.max())

    return False, current_max


def coordinate_descent(p0, deadline, top_k=200, n_grid=12, n_golden=12,
                       beta=80.0, no_improve_seconds=120.0,
                       recompute_every=50, seed=1):
    """
    Fast exact coordinate descent.

    deadline is an absolute time.monotonic() deadline.
    """
    p = normalize_p(p0)
    n = len(p)
    b = conv_self(p)
    best_max = float(b.max())
    best_score = float(2 * n * best_max)
    best_p = p.copy()

    last_improve = time.monotonic()
    updates = 0
    beta_cur = float(beta)
    no_progress_sweeps = 0
    max_p = float(p.max())
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        if updates > 0 and updates % recompute_every == 0:
            s = float(p.sum())
            if s > 0.0:
                p /= s
            b = conv_self(p)
            updates = 0

        bmax = float(b.max())
        if bmax < best_max - 1e-14:
            best_max = bmax
            best_score = float(2 * n * best_max)
            best_p = p.copy()
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
        elif bmax <= best_max + 1e-12:
            # Allow flat progress without resetting no-improve timer forever.
            last_improve = time.monotonic()

        if time.monotonic() - last_improve > no_improve_seconds:
            break

        w, L = lse_weights(b, beta_cur)
        g = conv_revp_w(p, w)
        g -= L
        g -= float(np.dot(p, g))
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

        k = int(min(n, top_k))
        order = np.abs(g)
        if k < n:
            part = np.argpartition(order, k - 1)[:k]
            top = part[np.argsort(order[part])[::-1]]
        else:
            top = np.argsort(order)[::-1][:k]
        top = rng.permutation(top)

        improved_this_sweep = False
        for idx in top:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
                break
            i = int(idx)
            x0 = float(p[i])
            upper = coord_upper(x0, max_p, n)
            improved, new_max = optimize_coordinate(p, b, i, upper, n_grid, n_golden)
            if improved:
                improved_this_sweep = True
                updates += 1
                if new_max < best_max - 1e-14:
                    best_max = new_max
                    best_score = float(2 * n * new_max)
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    last_improve = time.monotonic()
                elif new_max <= best_max + 1e-12:
                    last_improve = time.monotonic()

                if updates % 50 == 0:
                    max_p = float(p.max())

        if improved_this_sweep:
            no_progress_sweeps = 0
            beta_cur = min(20000.0, beta_cur * 1.25)
        else:
            no_progress_sweeps += 1
            beta_cur = min(40000.0, beta_cur * 1.5)
            if no_progress_sweeps >= 3:
                break

        if updates > 0 and updates % recompute_every == 0:
            s = float(p.sum())
            if s > 0.0:
                p /= s
            b = conv_self(p)
            updates = 0

    s = float(p.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        p /= s
    b = conv_self(p)
    sc = float(2 * n * b.max())
    if sc < best_score:
        best_score = sc
        best_p = p.copy()

    return best_score, best_p


def perturb_p(p, rng, kind, strength=0.1):
    """Return a diversified normalized candidate."""
    p = normalize_p(p)
    n = len(p)
    q = p.copy()

    if kind == 0:
        # Multiplicative additive noise.
        q = q * (1.0 + strength * rng.standard_normal(n))
        q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
        s = float(q.sum())
        if s > 0.0:
            q /= s
        else:
            return p.copy()

    elif kind == 1:
        # DFT-domain amplitude/phase perturbation.
        fft_len = 1 << (n - 1).bit_length()
        A = np.fft.rfft(q, fft_len)
        amp = 1.0 + strength * rng.standard_normal(len(A))
        amp = np.clip(amp, 0.25, 2.5)
        ph = 0.4 * strength * rng.standard_normal(len(A))
        A *= amp * np.exp(1j * ph)
        q = np.fft.irfft(A, fft_len)[:n]
        q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
        s = float(q.sum())
        if s <= 1e-20:
            return p.copy()
        q /= s

    elif kind == 2:
        # Block swaps.
        for _ in range(3):
            bl_lo = max(1, n // 300)
            bl_hi = max(bl_lo + 1, n // 24)
            bl = int(rng.integers(bl_lo, bl_hi + 1))
            i = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - bl)))
            j = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - bl)))
            if i != j and abs(i - j) >= bl:
                tmp = q[i:i + bl].copy()
                q[i:i + bl] = q[j:j + bl]
                q[j:j + bl] = tmp
        s = float(q.sum())
        if s > 0.0:
            q /= s

    elif kind == 3:
        # Anti-palindromic perturbation.
        u = rng.standard_normal(n)
        u = u - u[::-1]
        amp = strength * max(1e-4, float(p.max()))
        q = p + amp * u
        q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
        s = float(q.sum())
        if s <= 1e-20:
            return p.copy()
        q /= s

    else:
        return p.copy()

    return normalize_p(q)


def generate_fallback_parent(n, rng):
    """Used only if no parent sequence is available."""
    cand = []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    cand.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    for r in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        cand.append(r + x)
    for c in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        cand.append(np.exp(-c * x))
    for c in (1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        cand.append(np.exp(-c * x) + np.exp(-c * (1.0 - x)))
    # Two separated blocks.
    for gap in (0.15, 0.3):
        left_lo, left_hi = 0, (1.0 - gap) / 2.0
        right_lo, right_hi = (1.0 + gap) / 2.0, 1.0
        q = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        q[(x >= left_lo) & (x <= left_hi)] = 1.0
        q[(x >= right_lo) & (x <= right_hi)] = 1.0
        if q.sum() > 0:
            cand.append(q)

    best = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    for a in cand:
        q = normalize_p(a)
        s = score_p(q)
        if s < best_score:
            best_score = s
            best = q.copy()
    return best


def final_clean(p, best_score):
    """Test zeroing/trimming endpoints that can lower the effective length."""
    p = normalize_p(p)
    best_q = p.copy()
    best_sc = float(best_score)
    maxp = float(p.max())

    thresholds = (0.0, 1e-14 * maxp, 1e-12 * maxp, 1e-10 * maxp, 1e-8 * maxp)
    for thr in thresholds:
        q = p.copy()
        q[q < thr] = 0.0
        nz = np.nonzero(q > 0.0)[0]
        if nz.size == 0:
            continue
        q = q[nz[0]:nz[-1] + 1]
        if q.size < 2:
            continue
        q = normalize_p(q)
        s = score_p(q)
        if s < best_sc:
            best_sc = s
            best_q = q.copy()
    return best_sc, best_q


def propose_candidate(seed=4242, budget_s=2400.0):
    """
    Main search entry point.

    Runs exact-coordinate local optimization from the supplied parent,
    optional diversified restarts, resampling, and final polishing.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    effective_budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 60.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    # Load parent if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        hs = height_sequence_1
        if hs is not None:
            par = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            par = np.nan_to_num(par, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            par = np.clip(par, 0.0, None)
            if par.size >= 16 and float(par.sum()) > 1e-8:
                parent = par
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        parent = generate_fallback_parent(8192, rng)
        print("No parent available; using generated fallback parent.")
    else:
        if len(parent) > 15000:
            parent = resample_p(parent, 12000)
        elif len(parent) < 2048:
            parent = resample_p(parent, 4096)

    best_p = normalize_p(parent)
    best_score = score_p(best_p)
    print(f"Initial: n={len(best_p)}, score={best_score:.8f}")

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 1: deep exact-coordinate refinement from the parent.
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    phase1_deadline = t_start + effective_budget * 0.45
    if time.monotonic() < phase1_deadline - 10.0:
        s1, p1 = coordinate_descent(
            best_p,
            deadline=phase1_deadline,
            top_k=260,
            n_grid=12,
            n_golden=12,
            beta=80.0,
            no_improve_seconds=90.0,
            recompute_every=40,
            seed=101
        )
        if s1 < best_score:
            best_score = s1
            best_p = p1
            print(f"After phase1 CD: score={best_score:.8f}, n={len(best_p)}, "
                  f"t={time.monotonic() - t_start:.0f}s")

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 2: diversification.  A resample plus a few perturbations.
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    restart_deadline = t_start + effective_budget * 0.75

    # Growing the length often helps move toward the continuous limit.
    if time.monotonic() < restart_deadline - 160.0:
        new_len = min(12000, int(round(len(best_p) * 1.18)) + 1)
        if new_len > len(best_p) + 50:
            res_p = resample_p(best_p, new_len)
            res_sc = score_p(res_p)
            print(f"Resample candidate: n={new_len}, score={res_sc:.8f}")
            if res_sc < best_score * 1.15 + 1e-9:
                ddl = min(time.monotonic() + 210.0, restart_deadline - 90.0)
                rs2, rp2 = coordinate_descent(
                    res_p,
                    deadline=ddl,
                    top_k=220,
                    n_grid=10,
                    n_golden=10,
                    beta=250.0,
                    no_improve_seconds=30.0,
                    recompute_every=35,
                    seed=7272
                )
                if rs2 < best_score:
                    best_score = rs2
                    best_p = rp2
                    print(f"After resample CD: score={best_score:.8f}, "
                          f"n={len(best_p)}, t={time.monotonic() - t_start:.0f}s")

    # Small set of restarts.  We only spend serious time on candidates whose
    # initial score is not catastrophically bad.
    kinds = (0, 2, 1, 3, 0, 1, 2, 3)
    for r in range(8):
        rem = restart_deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0
        if rem < 50.0:
            break
        alloc = rem / float(8 - r)
        alloc = min(alloc, 170.0)
        if alloc < 45.0:
            break

        kind = kinds[r % len(kinds)]
        strength = 0.05 + 0.05 * (r % 4)
        q = perturb_p(best_p, rng, kind, strength)
        q_sc = score_p(q)
        print(f"Restart {r}: kind={kind}, init_score={q_sc:.8f}, "
              f"t={time.monotonic() - t_start:.0f}s")

        if q_sc < best_score * 1.35 + 1e-8:
            cd_deadline = time.monotonic() + 0.65 * alloc
            cd_deadline = min(cd_deadline, restart_deadline - 20.0)
            if cd_deadline > time.monotonic() + 25.0:
                rs, rp = coordinate_descent(
                    q,
                    deadline=cd_deadline,
                    top_k=160,
                    n_grid=10,
                    n_golden=10,
                    beta=300.0,
                    no_improve_seconds=18.0,
                    recompute_every=30,
                    seed=9000 + 17 * r
                )
                if rs < best_score:
                    best_score = rs
                    best_p = rp
                    print(f"Restart {r} new best: score={best_score:.8f}, "
                          f"n={len(best_p)}, t={time.monotonic() - t_start:.0f}s")

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 3: final intensive polish of the best sequence.
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    final_deadline = t_start + effective_budget - 35.0
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 25.0:
        s3, p3 = coordinate_descent(
            best_p,
            deadline=final_deadline,
            top_k=450,
            n_grid=13,
            n_golden=13,
            beta=900.0,
            no_improve_seconds=10000.0,
            recompute_every=35,
            seed=31337
        )
        if s3 < best_score:
            best_score = s3
            best_p = p3
            print(f"Final CD: score={best_score:.8f}, n={len(best_p)}, "
                  f"t={time.monotonic() - t_start:.0f}s")

    # Zeroing/trimming can remove useless length and sometimes small diagonal mass.
    best_score, best_p = final_clean(best_p, best_score)
    if best_score < float("inf"):
        print(f"After final clean: score={best_score:.8f}, n={len(best_p)}, "
              f"t={time.monotonic() - t_start:.0f}s")

    # One short last-moment polish if cleaning changed the candidate and we have time.
    if time.monotonic() < t_start + effective_budget - 20.0:
        s4, p4 = coordinate_descent(
            best_p,
            deadline=t_start + effective_budget - 10.0,
            top_k=260,
            n_grid=10,
            n_golden=10,
            beta=1500.0,
            no_improve_seconds=5.0,
            recompute_every=25,
            seed=555
        )
        if s4 < best_score:
            best_score = s4
            best_p = p4
            best_score, best_p = final_clean(best_p, best_score)

    out = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        out = np.array([0.5], dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        out /= s

    out_list = [float(x) for x in out]
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
        if verified < best_score:
            best_score = verified
        print(f"Final: internal={best_score:.8f}, verified={verified:.8f}, "
              f"n={len(out_list)}, time={time.monotonic() - t_start:.1f}s")
    except Exception:
        print(f"Final: internal={best_score:.8f}, n={len(out_list)}, "
              f"time={time.monotonic() - t_start:.1f}s")

    return out_list
```
